------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calculadesconto
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calculadesconto
Lparameters pValor,pDescNP, pAcrescimo

Local ValDsc,ValBcd

Select xPar
Sum ValDescs to ValDsc
Sum Valos + ValDescs to ValBcd For ValDescs <> 0
Go Bottom

If pDescNP
	If Not ThisForm.DescnPar
		Return .t.
	EndIf
	ThisForm.Get_VDescP.Value = ValDsc
	Replace ValVarPs With ValDsc * -1 in TprMvCab
	ThisForm.Bcds = ValBcd
Else
	If pAcrescimo
		ThisForm.Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value = ValDsc + pValor
		ThisForm.Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.InteractiveChange()
	Else
		ThisForm.Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value = ValDsc + pValor
		ThisForm.Desconto.Get_TotVariação.InteractiveChange()
	Endif
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calculasaldo
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calculasaldo
Local lnIcm,lnIpi,lntVar,lnSaldo,lnpvar,lnvvar,_total,_varia

With ThisForm

	lnIcm = 0
	lnIpi = 0
	Select CrSigMvNfi
	Go Top
	If !crSigMvNfi.Cancelas
		lnIpi = crSigMvNfi.ValIpis
		If ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.cursorquery('SigCdCfo','CrSigCdCfo','codigos',CrSigMvNfi.Cfis)
			If crSigCdCfo.IncIcmNfs = 1
				lnIcm = crSigMvNfi.Valicms
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf

	lnTVar  = (.Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Get_vdescP.Value ) * -1
	lnTran  = Iif(crSigOpCdc.nChkVsts = 1, TprMvCab.ValTrans, 0)
	
	lnSaldo = (.Get_Valinic.Value + .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value + ;
			   TprMvCab.ValEncs + TprMvCab.ValServs + lnIpi + lnIcm - .Get_vdescP.Value + ;
				CrSigMvNfi.VlFretes + CrSigMvNfi.Vlseguros + CrSigMvNfi.DespAces + CrSigMvNfi.Icmss + lnTran)

	lnTC    = .Get_ValTot.Value + .get_vDescp.Value
	lnpVar  = Iif(.Get_ValTot.Value = 0,0,Round( .Get_VdescP.Value / lnTC * 100, 4 ) )
		
	.Get_pDescP.Value   = lnpVar
	.Get_Saldo.Value    = lnSaldo - .Get_TotForma.Value
	.Get_Variação.Value = lnTvar
	.Get_Saldo.Refresh
	.Get_Variação.Refresh
	.Get_pDescP.Refresh
	.Get_TotServiço.Refresh

	If crSigCdOpe.Vars <> 5
		lnvVar = .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value - .acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value + .Get_vdescP.Value

		xServ = Iif(CrSigMvNfi.vTotSers <> 0,CrSigMvNfi.vTotSers,TprMvCab.ValServs)
		_Total = Iif(CrSigCdOpe.Vars = 4,ThisForm.ItSemBonus-TprMvCab.valdevs,TprMvCab.Valinis) + xServ

		_Varia = IIf(_Total=0,0,Round( lnvVar / _Total * 100, 4 ))

		*** Tiago - 11/05/2011 - Se variação = 1 e valor de variação padrão estiver preenchido
		If _Varia = 0 and (crSigCdOpe.vars = 1 And crSigOpCdc.VarPads <> 0)
			_Varia = crSigOpCdc.VarPads * -1
		EndIf	

		Replace ValVars With -lnvVar ,;
				Vars    With -_Varia in TprMvCab
	EndIf
Endwith

Return lnSaldo
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE enviatef
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE enviatef
*!*	LOCAL RetImpr, llOkTef, lcTipo, lcNSU, lcIdents

*!*	*** Mena 20/05/2013 - metodo incluido para envio de tef quando pagamento em cartao ***

*!*	=CarregaImpfis(CrSigOpCdd.NomEcfs)
*!*	RetImpr = .t.
*!*	llOkTef = .t.
*!*	SET STEP ON 
*!*	If crSigCdOpe.cupfis=3 and !Empty(crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs) And !Empty(xpar.valos) &&	mena 18/07/2016 and CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA'
*!*		Wait Window 'Aguarde processamento TEF' nowait

*!*		=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigFiNfs','crSigFiNfs','cComprovs',CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
*!*		Select crSigFiNfs
*!*		Locate For Imps = CrSigFiFis.Imps
*!*		If Not Eof()
*!*			Select crSigFiNfs
*!*			Do Case
*!*				Case crSigFiNfs.nFinalzs=1
*!*					lcTipo = 'CHQ'
*!*						RetImpr = .t.
*!*						SendTef = VerifGP('ATV', thisform, '', '', CrSigFiFis.Imps, Thisform.ParentForm.poDatamgr, 'TEF' )
*!*						RetCrtChq = TefChq(TprMvCab.Emps,TprMvCab.Dopes,lcTipo,SendTef,Val(TprMvCab.Notas),xpar.vpags,;
*!*						                    "","","","","","","","","","","",xpar.vpags )
*!*				Case crSigFiNfs.nFinalzs=2
*!*					lcTipo = 'CRT' 
*!*				Case crSigFiNfs.nFinalzs=3
*!*					lcTipo = 'PRE' 
*!*				OtherWise
*!*					Return .t.
*!*			EndCase
*!*	**					If Thisform.parentform.TransTef = 1 And !ThisForm.Tef And crSigCdPam.Sistef = 1
*!*			If Thisform.parentform.TransTef = 0 And crSigCdPam.Sistef = 1
*!*				=ApagarIntpos(ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr)	&& apaga tudo - mena - 05/2012
*!*			endif
*!*			If Thisform.parentform.TransTef > 0 And crSigCdPam.Sistef = 1
*!*				lcIdents='R'+TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+alltrim(str(Thisform.parentform.IdentTef,20))
*!*				ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)

*!*				**** salva nsu para verificar se crt já confirmado ****
*!*				lcNsu = crSigFiTef.cnNSUs
*!*				lcQuery = [Select * from SigFiTef Where cnnsus = '] + Alltrim(lcNsu) + [' And ]+;
*!*				[cTipos = 'E' and cheaders = 'CNF']
*!*				ThisForm.Parentform.poDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'TmpAux3')

*!*				SELECT * from TmpAux3 where ;
*!*				VAL(Strtran(ALLTRIM(cnhrtrans), [ ], [0])) >= VAL(Strtran(ALLTRIM(crSigFiTef.cnhrtrans), [ ], [0])) ;
*!*				order by cemps,cdopes,cnidents desc into cursor tmpAux readwrite

*!*				IF RECCOUNT('tmpaux') = 0		&& envia CNF se ainda não confirmou	
*!*					*** Ativa gp Mena 05/2012 ****
*!*					SendTef = VerifGP('ATV', thisform, '', '', CrSigFiFis.Imps, Thisform.ParentForm.poDatamgr, 'TEF' )
*!*					ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)
*!*					lcTipoOK = 'CNF'
*!*					RetImpr=TefImpr(TprMvCab.Emps,TprMvCab.Dopes,TprMvCab.Numes,lcTipoOk,;
*!*									val(crSigFiTef.cnIdents),crSigFiTef.cNmRedes,crSigFiTef.cnNSUs,;
*!*									crSigFiTef.cFinalzs,crSigFiTef.cCupomnvs,CrSigFiTef.Fpags)
*!*					=apagaResp(ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr)	&& apaga intpos.001 - mena - 05/2012
*!*				endif
*!*			EndIf
*!*			If RetImpr And llOkTef
*!*				If lcTipo = 'CHQ'
*!*					Thisform.parentform.IdentTef = SendTef
*!*					Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.fweditdata_venc.setfocus
*!*					Return .t.
*!*				Else
*!*					lnIdent = 0
*!*					lcNSus = ''
*!*					lnOrdem = crSigFiNfs.nOrdems
*!*					SendTef = VerifGP('ATV', thisform, '', '', CrSigFiFis.Imps, Thisform.ParentForm.poDatamgr, 'TEF' )
*!*					=ApagaResp(ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr)	&& apaga intpos.001 anterior
*!*					SendTef = SigFiTefReq(xpar.fpags,Thisform.parentform.NoCaixa,TprMvCab.Emps,TprMvCab.Dopes,TprMvCab.Numes,xpar.valos,;
*!*										lcTipo,lnOrdem,crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,;
*!*										xpar.valos,.f.,.f.,xpar.fpags,;
*!*										.f.,.f.,.f.,ThisForm)
*!*					If Type('SendTef')='N'
*!*						=FazBkpIntpos(ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr)	&& Faz bkp do confirmado - mena - 05/2012
*!*						lnIdent = SendTef
*!*						lcIdents='R'+TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+alltrim(str(lnIdent,20))
*!*						IF ThisForm.Parentform.poDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)
*!*							lcNSus = crSigFiTef.cnNSUs
*!*						Else
*!*							Messagebox('Erro Resposta Tef !!',0+48,'ERRO')
*!*							llOkTef = .f.
*!*						Endif
*!*						lcIdents='R'+TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+alltrim(str(lnIdent,20))
*!*						ThisForm.Parentform.poDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)
*!*						lcTipoOK = 'CNF'

*!*						If ( lcTipo = 'CRT' or lcTipo = 'PRE' ) And llOkTef
*!*							Thisform.parentform.TransTef = Thisform.parentform.TransTef + 1
*!*						Endif
*!*						Thisform.parentform.IdentTef = lnIdent
*!*						replace cnIdTefs With alltrim(str(lnIdent,10)) In xPar
*!*						=AjustaFp()
*!*					Else
*!*						SELECT xPar
*!*						DELETE
*!*						GO bottom
*!*						llOkTef = .f.
*!*					EndIf
*!*				Endif
*!*			Else
*!*				llOkTef = .f.
*!*			Endif
*!*		Endif
*!*		WAIT Clear 
*!*	Endif

*!*	Select xPar
*!*	Return (llOkTef)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE fpagsauto
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE fpagsauto
Local lcCta

If InList(ThisForm.ParentForm.pcEscolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR')
	Select FPags ;
	  From xPar ;
	 Where Not Empty(FPags) ;
	  Into Cursor LocalPar

	Go Top In LocalPar
	If Eof('LocalPar')
		lcCta = Iif(crSigCdOpe.COpers = 1, TprMvCab.ContaDs, TprMvCab.ContaOs)
		
		ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli', 'LocalCli', 'IClis', lcCta, 'FPags')
		If Used('LocalCli') And Not Eof('LocalCli') And Not Empty(LocalCli.FPags)
			lcQuery = [Select a.Fpags, a.Descrs, a.MoeFpgs, a.Fparcs, o.Obrigs, o.Dopes, a.cCupomNvs ] + ;
						[From SigOpFp a, SigOpFpo o ] + ;
					   [Where a.Fpags = o.Fpags ] + ;
					   [Order By a.FPags]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalTmpFPag') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalTmpFPag)')
				Return 0
			EndIf

			Select Fpags, Descrs, MoeFpgs, Fparcs, Obrigs ;
			  From LocalTmpFpag ;
			 Where Dopes = TprMvCab.Dopes And ;
				   Not Fpags = crSigCdOpe.FpagOpCrs ;
			  Into Cursor LocalSigOpFp

			Select LocalSigOpFp
			Go Top
			If Eof('LocalSigOpFp')
				lcQuery = [Select Fpags, Descrs, MoeFpgs, Fparcs, 0 as Obrigs, cCupomNvs, Situas ] + ;
							[From SigOpFp ] + ;
						   [Order By FPags]

				If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalTmpFPag') < 1)
					=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalTmpFPag)')
					Return 0
				EndIf

				vCond = Iif(Inlist(crSigCdOpe.Copers, 1, 2), 'Inlist(Situas, "A", " ", "' + ;
						Iif(crSigCdOpe.Copers=1, 'R', 'P') + '")', '.t.')

				Select Fpags, Descrs, MoeFpgs, Fparcs, Obrigs ;
				  From LocalTmpFpag ;
				 Where &vCond. And ;
					   FPags <> crSigCdOpe.FPagOpCrs ;
				  Into Cursor LocalSigOpFp
			EndIf
		
			Select LocalSigOpFp
			Go Top
			Locate For FPags = LocalCli.FPags
			If Not Eof('LocalSigOpFp')
				ThisForm.get_Fpg.Value = LocalCli.FPags
				If Not ThisForm.get_Fpg.Valid()
					Thisform.Get_Fpg.Value = ' '
					Return .f.
				Endif
				
				If Empty(ThisForm.get_NPar.Value)
					ThisForm.get_NPar.Value = 1
				EndIf
				ThisForm.get_NPar.Valid()
				
				ThisForm.get_VPar.SetFocus
				Keyboard '{ENTER}'
				Keyboard '{ENTER}'
				Keyboard '{ENTER}'
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE inchequeserial
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE inchequeserial
Thisform.Leitor = .F.
If Thisform.ReadComm.Enabled 
	Thisform.ReadComm.leitura()
	Thisform.LeituraSerial = Thisform.readcomm.campo
	Thisform.CntCheque.Get_Bco.Valid()
	If Thisform.Leitor
		If Not Empty(xpar.bancos)
			If Not fChkCheques(xpar.bancos,xpar.agencias,xpar.contas,xpar.numeros,ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr,.f.,xPar.Dopes)
				Replace bancos With'', agencias With'', contas With'', numeros With''
				Thisform.LeituraSerial = ''
				With Thisform.CntCheque
					.Get_Bco.Value = ''
					.Get_Age.Value = ''
					.Get_Conta.Value = ''
					.Get_Num.Value = ''
					.Get_Bco.SetFocus
				EndWith
			Else
				Thisform.CntCheque.Get_Num.SetFocus	
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Else
		Thisform.CntCheque.Get_Age.SetFocus
	EndIf
	Thisform.Leitor = .F.
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE limpa_che
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE limpa_che
Local ldVenc

ldVenc = Iif(Not Empty(ThisForm.Dataget_venc.Value), ThisForm.Dataget_venc.Value, TprMvCab.Datas )
Thisform.GetProts = .F.
Select xPar
Replace xPar.Bancos   With Space(3), ;
		xPar.Agencias With Space(4), ;
		xPar.Contas   With Space(10), ;
		xPar.Numeros  With Space(6)

Replace xPar.adquirente With Space(15),;
		xPar.autoriza 	With Space(6),;
		xPar.nsu 		With Space(15)
		
If xPar.InsChs
	Replace xPar.Valos With 0,      ;
			xPar.Vencs With ldVenc, ;
			xPar.InsChs With .F.		
EndIf

With Thisform.CntCheque
	.Get_Bco.Refresh
	.Get_Age.Refresh
	.Get_Conta.Refresh
	.Get_Num.Refresh
	.Get_ProtChq.Value = ''
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mgetcssigfpag
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mgetcssigfpag
Parameters plMasc

Local lcCampos, lnCount, lcMoeda, lcFiltro, lcFiltro2

lcFiltro = []
lcFiltro2 = []
If plMasc
	lcMod = Alltrim(Upper(ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getModalidade.Value ))

	lcFiltro = lcFiltro + [ And a.ModPagto = ']+lcMod+[' ]
	
	If lcMod = 'CARTÃO'
		*-- Formas 
		If Not Empty(ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getFormas.Value)
			lcFiltro = lcFiltro + [ And a.Formas = ']+ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getFormas.Value+[' ]
		EndIf 
		*-- Adquirente
		If Not Empty(ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getAdquirente.Value)
			lcFiltro = lcFiltro + [ And a.Adquirente = ']+ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getAdquirente.Value+[' ]
		EndIf 
		
		*-- bandeira
		If Not Empty(ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getBandeira.Value)
			lcFiltro = lcFiltro + [ And a.Bandeira = ']+ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getBandeira.Value+[' ]
		EndIf 
		
		*--Numero de Parcelas
		If Not Empty(ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getParcela.Value)
			If ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getParcela.Value = 1
				lcFiltro = lcFiltro + [ And a.FParcs = 1 ]
			Else
				lcFiltro = lcFiltro + [ And ] + Alltrim(Str(ThisForm.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getParcela.Value)) + [ BetWeen a.ParcIni And a.Parcfin ]
			EndIf  
		EndIf 
	EndIf 	
	lcFiltro2 = Strtran(lcFiltro,[a.],[])	
EndIf 

llPerInativ = .T.
lcMoeda  = Thisform.Get_modfpg.Value
=ThisForm.Parentform.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
=ThisForm.Parentform.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpCdd','CrSigOpCdd','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
=ThisForm.Parentform.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpCdI','CrSigOpCdI','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

If Type([crSigOpCdi.PgtoInativ])=[N] And crSigOpCdi.PgtoInativ<>1
	llPerInativ = .F.
EndIf 

*** mena 18/03/2017 - checa grupo empresarial ***
If InList(Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') Or Thisform.AltDireta
	LcQuery = [Select a.Fpags,a.Descrs,a.MoeFpgs,a.Fparcs,o.Obrigs,o.Dopes,a.cCupomNvs, ]+;
			  [a.GrupoDs,a.ContaDs,a.ValPends, a.AgCobs, a.OriDes, a.BlqTabDs,a.opcsnas, ]+;
			  [a.bandeira, a.chkBlqBon ]+;
			  [From SigOpFp a, SigOpFpo o ] + ;
			  [Where a.Fpags = o.Fpags And ] + ;
			  		[o.Fpagss = Space(12) And ] + ;
			  		[a.Emps In ('] + TprMvCab.Emps + [', '   ') and a.grupoemp in (']+crSigcdemp.grupo+[','          ') ] + ;
			  Iif(Not IsEmpty(lcMoeda),[And a.MoeFpgs = ']+lcMoeda+[' ],[ ]) + ;
			  Iif(Not llPerInativ,[And a.Situas <> 'I' ],[ ]) + lcFiltro
	If Thisform.Parentform.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpag') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf

	Select Fpags, Descrs, MoeFpgs, Fparcs, Obrigs, Grupods, Contads, ValPends, AgCobs, OriDes, BlqTabDs, OpcSnas, bandeira, chkBlqBon ;
	From CrTmpFpag Where  ;
			Dopes = TprMvCab.Dopes And Not Fpags = CrSigCdOpe.FpagOpCrs ;
	Into Cursor LocalFpag

	Select LocalFpag
	Go Top
	If Eof('LocalFpag')
		LcQuery = [Select Fpags, Descrs, MoeFpgs, Fparcs, 0 as Obrigs, cCupomNvs, Situas, ]+;
				  [Grupods, Contads, ValPends, AgCobs, OriDes, BlqTabDs, OpcSnas, bandeira, chkBlqBon ] + ;
				  [From SigOpFp ]+;
				  [Where Emps In ('] + TprMvCab.Emps + [', '   ') and grupoemp in (']+crSigcdemp.grupo+[','          ') ]+;
				  Iif(Empty(lcMoeda), [], [ And MoeFpgs = ']+lcMoeda+[']) + lcFiltro2

		If Thisform.Parentform.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpag') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		vCond = Iif(Inlist(CrSigCdOpe.Copers, 1, 2), 'Inlist(Situas, "A", " ", "' + ;
				Iif(CrSigCdOpe.Copers=1, 'R', 'P') + ;
				Iif(CrSigCdOpe.Copers=1 And CrSigCdOpe.Caixas=1, ',S', ' ') + '")',Iif(llPerInativ,'.t.',[Situas <> 'I'])) &&

		Select Fpags, Descrs, MoeFpgs, Fparcs, Obrigs, Grupods, Contads, ValPends, AgCobs, OriDes, BlqTabDs, OpcSnas, bandeira, chkBlqBon;
		From CrTmpFpag ;
			Where &vCond. And ;
			FPags # CrSigCdOpe.FPagOpCrs ;
		Into Cursor LocalFpag
	EndIf
	
	lcSql = [Select Distinct fpags From SigOpFpD Where Tabds = ']+TprMvCab.TabDs+[']
	If Thisform.parentform.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'LocalFpagD') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	If Reccount('LocalfpagD') > 0
		Select a.* From LocalFpag a, LocalFpagD b Where Iif(InList(CrSigOpCdd.OpcSnas,1,2),a.OpcSnas = CrSigOpCdd.OpcSnas,;
		Iif(CrSigOpCdd.OpcSNas = 4, InList(a.OpcSnas,1,2),.t.) ) ;
		And a.Fpags = b.fpags ;
		Into Cursor CsSigOpFp ReadWrite
	Else
		Select * From LocalFpag Where Iif(InList(CrSigOpCdd.OpcSnas,1,2),OpcSnas = CrSigOpCdd.OpcSnas,;
		Iif(CrSigOpCdd.OpcSNas = 4, InList(OpcSnas,1,2),.t.) ) ;
		Into Cursor CsSigOpFp ReadWrite
	Endif

	*** Mena - modulo desconto - Se tem o desconto financeiro não permite forma de pagamento pendente ***
	select * from crSigMvItd where tpdesc = 2 into cursor crAux1
	IF !EOF('crAux1')
		lcSql = [Select codigos From SigOpTdt]
		If Thisform.parentform.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'crTdt') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		select * from crAux1 where cdesc in (select codigos from crTdt) into cursor crAux2
		IF EOF('crAux2')
			DELETE from csSigopfp where fparcs<>1	&& mena - 27/06/2017 - à vista =  numero de parcelas = 1
		ENDIF
	ENDIF
 
Else
	LcQuery = [Select Fpags, Descrs, MoeFpgs, Fparcs, 0 as Obrigs, BlqTabDs, bandeira, chkBlqBon ] + ;
				[From SigOpFp ]+;
				[Where Emps In ('] + TprMvCab.Emps + [', '   ') and grupoemp in (']+crSigcdemp.grupo+[','          ') ]+;
			   		  Iif(Empty(lcMoeda), [], [ And MoeFpgs = ']+lcMoeda+[' ])
			   		  
	If Thisform.parentform.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CsSigOpFp') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
EndIf

Select csSigOpFp
Index On Fpags Tag Fpags
Go Top
Count To lnCount For Obrigs = 1
Thisform.plFPagObrigs = Iif( lnCount # 0, .T., .F. )
Return (.t.)

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mostraobs
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mostraobs
Local lcInfos, lcFPags, llProts, lnChqOpers, lnCartOpers, llAprova

With Thisform
	.CntCheque.Visible    = .F.
	.CntCCreditoE.Visible = .F.
	.CntCCreditoS.Visible = .F.
	.CntCarne.Visible     = .F.
	.CntObs.Visible       = .F.
	.GetObs.Visible       = .F.
	.CntNsu.Visible       = .F.		&& mena - 25/02/2016 incluido este container para pegar os numeros NSU e Autorização quando cartao
EndWith

Select xPar
If Not Eof() And Not Bof()
	lcFPags = xPar.FPags
	=ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',lcFpags)
	lcInfos  = CrSigOpFp.Infos
	llAprova = ( CrSigOpFp.Aprovas = 1 )

	lcInf = crSigOpFp.Infos
	lcBan = crSigOpFp.Bancos
	lcAge = crSigOpFp.Agencias
	lcCon = crSigOpFp.NContas
	lcChq = ThisForm.NewChq
	lcEmi = (crSigOpFp.EmiChqs = 1 And ThisForm.EntraCheque And ;
			Not IsEmpty(lcBan) And Not IsEmpty(lcAge) And Not IsEmpty(lcCon) )

	ThisForm.EntraCheque = (lcEmi) .and. (lcInf='C')

	ThisForm.GetObs.Visible = InList(CrSigOpFp.Obs,1,3)
	ThisForm.GetObs.Enabled = InList(CrSigOpFp.Obs,1,3)	&& mena - 14/07/2016 - habilita este campo Rosana
	ThisForm.GetObs.refresh


	Select xPar

	Do Case
		Case lcInfos = 'C'
			With ThisForm
				.cntApanhaChq.visible = .F.
				.cntApanhaChq.Enabled = .F.
				For I = 1 to .cntApanhaChq.ControlCount
				   .cntApanhaChq.Objects[I].Visible = .cntApanhaChq.Visible
				   .cntApanhaChq.Objects[I].Enabled = .cntApanhaChq.Enabled
				Next
				If lcEmi
					.cntApanhaChq.Visible = .T.
					.cntApanhaChq.Enabled = .T.
					For I = 1 to .cntApanhaChq.ControlCount
					   .cntApanhaChq.Objects[I].Enabled = .cntApanhaChq.Enabled
					   .cntApanhaChq.Objects[I].Visible = .cntApanhaChq.Visible
					Next
					.cntApanhaChq.getBanco.Value = lcBan
					.cntApanhaChq.getAgencia.Value = lcAge
					.cntApanhaChq.getConta.Value = lcCon
					.cntApanhaChq.getNumero.Value = lcChq
					.cntApanhaChq.Refresh
				Else
					.CntCheque.Visible = .T.
					With ThisForm.CntCheque
						lnChqOpers = Iif( (crSigCdOpe.COpers = 1 And CrSigOpFp.Trocos = 1) Or ;
										  (crSigCdOpe.COpers = 2 And CrSigOpFp.Trocos # 1), 2, 1)
						llProts    = InList(Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR') And (lnChqOpers = 2)
						.Get_ProtChq.Visible   = InList(Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR', 'CONSULTAR', 'EXCLUIR') And (lnChqOpers = 2)
						.lbl_ProtChq.Visible   = InList(Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR', 'CONSULTAR', 'EXCLUIR') And (lnChqOpers = 2)
						.Get_ProtChq.Enabled   = llProts And Empty(xPar.Bancos + xPar.Agencias + xPar.Contas + xPar.Numeros)
						.Get_Aprovacao.Visible = Not llProts And llAprova
						.Say_Aprovacao.Visible = Not llProts And llAprova 
						.Get_Bco.Refresh
						.Get_Age.Refresh
						.Get_Conta.Refresh
						.Get_Num.Refresh
						If .Get_ProtChq.Visible And Not .Get_ProtChq.Enabled And InList(Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'CONSULTAR', 'EXCLUIR')
							.Get_ProtChq.Value = AllTrim(xPar.Outros)
						EndIf
						.Refresh
					EndWith
					Thisform.cFoco = Iif(llProts,'CntCheque.Get_ProtChq','')
				Endif
			EndWith

		Case lcInfos = 'T'
			lnCartOpers = Iif( (crSigCdOpe.COpers = 1 And CrSigOpFp.Trocos = 1) Or ;
						 	   (crSigCdOpe.COpers = 2 And CrSigOpFp.Trocos # 1), 2, 1)
			If lnCartOpers = 1
				With Thisform.CntCCreditoE
					.Visible = .T.
					.Get_Cartao.Refresh
					.Get_Cred.Refresh
					.Refresh
				EndWith
			Else
				With Thisform.CntCCreditoS
					.Visible = .T.
					.Get_Obs.Refresh
					.Refresh
				EndWith
				Thisform.cFoco = 'CntCCreditoS.Get_Obs'
			Endif

			*** Mena - 25/02/2016 incluido este container para pegar os numeros NSU e Autorização quando cartao ***
			If INLIST(CrSigOpFp.nsu,1,3)
				With Thisform.CntNsu
					.Visible = .T.
					.Get_Nsu.Refresh
					.Get_Autoriza.Refresh
					.Get_adquire.value=IIF(xpar.adquirente='        ',crSigOpFp.adquirente, xpar.adquirente)
					.Refresh
				EndWith
			endif
		
		Case lcInfos = 'N' Or CrSigOpFp.VlrFpgs > 0 And CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs = 8
			With Thisform.CntCarne
				.Visible = .T.
				.Say6.Caption = Iif(CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs = 8 and CrSigOpFp.VlrFpgs > 0,'Barra','Carne')
				.Get_Carne.Refresh
				.Refresh
			EndWith
			
		Case lcInfos = 'O'
			With Thisform.CntObs
				.Visible = .T.
				.Get_Obs.Refresh
				.Get_Aprovacao.Visible = llAprova
				.Say_Aprovacao.Visible = llAprova
				.Refresh
			EndWith

		Case lcInfos = 'B' And CrSigOpFp.impbols = 1
			With Thisform.CntCarne
				.Visible = .T.
				.Say6.Caption = 'No.Bancário'
				.Get_Carne.Refresh
				.Refresh
			EndWith
			Thisform.cFoco = 'CntCarne.Get_Carne'
	EndCase
Endif
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mtotalforma
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mtotalforma
Local cTotal, _RecNo, Cotacao, CotOper

cTotal=0
Select xPar
_RecNo = Recno()
Scan
	=ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','crSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
	Cotacao = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( xpar.moefpgs )
	CotOper = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( ThisForm.Get_MoeOper.Value )
	lnInd   = Iif(CrSigOpFp.Indices=0,1,CrSigOpFp.Indices)
	If CrSigOpFp.Trocos = 1
		cTotal = Round( cTotal - ( xPar.Valos * (xPar.CotFPgs*lnInd) / CotOper ), 2 )
	Else
		cTotal = Round( cTotal + ( xPar.Valos * (xPar.CotFPgs*lnInd) / CotOper ), 2 )
	Endif
ENDSCAN
*** Mena - 26/05/2014 - soma o valor dos impostos de serviço no recebimento ***
*** mena - 30/06/2014 - verifica se considera o imposto nO RECEBIMENTO ***
lcConta = crSigMvNfi.CliFors

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [ISS], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vtotiss, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [INSS], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vinss, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [IRRF], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.virf, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [CSSL], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vcsll, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [PIS], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vpis, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [COFINS], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr)
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vcofins, 0)

&&cTotal = cTotal + (crSigmvnfi.vcofins + crSigmvnfi.vcsll + crSigmvnfi.vinss + crSigmvnfi.virf + crSigmvnfi.vpis + crSigmvnfi.vtotiss)	&& mena 03/06/2014 valiis) 
ThisForm.Get_TotForma.Value = cTotal
ThisForm.Get_Saldo.Value    = ThisForm.Get_ValTot.Value - ThisForm.Get_TotForma.Value
Select xPar
Go _Recno

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE pagtoinv
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE pagtoinv
Select xPar
With Thisform
	.Get_TotForma.Value = .Get_TotForma.Value - xPar.valos
	.Get_Saldo.Value    = .Get_ValTot.Value - .Get_TotForma.Value
	.Get_NPar.Enabled = .t.
	.Get_VPar.Enabled = .t.
	.Get_Fpg.Enabled = .t.
	.Get_Fpg.Value = ''
	.Get_NPar.Value = 0
	.get_vpar.Value = 0
	.Dataget_venc.Value = {}
	.Get_Cotacao.Value = 0
	.GetPorc.Value = 0
	.Get_pVaria.Value = 0
	.Get_tVaria.Value = 0
	.get_fpg.SetFocus
	.Refresh
EndWith
Thisform.Limpa_Che()
Select xPar
Delete
Goto Top
Thisform.MostraObs()
Thisform.Refresh



ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE return
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE return
If Thisform.AltDireta

	=Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

	If crSigCdOpe.parcs=1

		Thisform.Parentform.TotPar()
		If !_Conserto And ( Thisform.Parentform.topar = 0 Or Thisform.Parentform.topar # TprMvCab.valos )
			If Not((Thisform.Parentform.topar - Thisform.Parentform.munipar) <= TprMvCab.valos And TprMvCab.valos <= (Thisform.Parentform.topar + Thisform.Parentform.munipar));
					or  Thisform.Parentform.munipar=0
				Messagebox('Forma de Pagamento Inválida !!',0+48,'')
				Return .F.
			Endif
		Endif
		If _Conserto And Thisform.Parentform.topar # TprMvCab.valos
			If Not((Thisform.Parentform.topar - Thisform.Parentform.munipar) <= TprMvCab.valos And TprMvCab.valos <= (Thisform.Parentform.topar + Thisform.Parentform.munipar));
					or Thisform.Parentform.munipar=0
				Messagebox('Forma de Pagamento Inválida !!',0+48,'')
				Return .F.
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif

	Select xPar
	Scan
		If Vencs < TprMvCab.DataS
			If (Type([ThisForm.Parentform.ChecouVencs]) = [L]) And Not Thisform.Parentform.ChecouVencs
				If (Messagebox([O Vencimento Está Menor Que a Data da Movimentação!] + Chr(13) + ;
						[Confirma o Lançamento Mesmo Com Essa Inconsistência?], 4+32+256, [Vencimento Menor Que o Movimento]) = 6)
					lcMsg = [O Usuário Confirmou a Gravação da Movimentação Com Vencimento Menor Que o Movimento]
					=fGravarLog([X], TprMvCab.Dopes, Thisform.Parentform.pcEscolha, lcMsg)
					Thisform.Parentform.ChecouVencs = .T.
				Else
					Return .F.
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif

		If crSigCdOpe.cheqs=1
		
			=Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigOpCdd],[crTmpOpp],[Dopes],xPar.Dopes,[AcmVlrCheq])
			
		
			=Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
			If CrSigOpFp.Infos = 'C'
				If Empty( Agencias ) Or Empty( Bancos ) Or Empty( Contas ) Or Empty( Numeros )
					=Messagebox( 'Cheque cadastro com dados inválidos!', 48, '' )
					Return .F.
				Else
					lcCheque = Bancos + Agencias + Contas + Numeros
					Declare LaCampo(4,2)
					LaCampo(1,1)='Bancos'
					LaCampo(2,1)='Agencias'
					LaCampo(3,1)='nContas'
					LaCampo(4,1)='Ncheques'
					LaCampo(1,2)=xPar.Bancos
					LaCampo(2,2)=xPar.Agencias
					LaCampo(3,2)=xPar.Contas
					LaCampo(4,2)=xPar.Numeros
					If Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigChe','CrCheque',@LaCampo)
						If Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCqChm','CrChm','Nums',CrCheque.Nums)
							If crTmpOpp.AcmVlrCheq = 1
								*-- Rafael - 07/02/2018 - Quando acumula valor na movimentação nao valida se o cheque ja esta cadastrado.
							Else
								Select CrChm
								Index On Codigos Tag Codigos
								Seek 'ENTRADA'
								
								If Emps + Dopes + Str( NumDopes, 6 ) # TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str( TprMvCab.Numes, 6 )
									=Messagebox( 'Cheque já cadastrado!', 48, '' )
									Return .F.
								EndIf
							EndIf 
						Endif
					Endif
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif
	Endscan

	Thisform.Parentform.Atuatot()

	*- Verifica Limite de Desconto -*
	llVerLimite = .F.
	If crSigCdOpe.Deacrs=1 And TprMvCab.Vars # 0
		llVerLimite = .T.
		lnValVars = TprMvCab.ValVars
		lnVars    = TprMvCab.Vars
		lnValos   = TprMvCab.valos
	Endif
	Limite = .T.
	If llVerLimite
		If CrSigOpCdd.VarPs = 1
			Select xPar
			Sum ValDescs To ValDsc
			lnvVaria = lnValVars + ValDsc
			lnvValo  = lnValos + ValDsc
			lnpVaria = Round(lnvVaria/TprMvCab.ValInis*100,4)
			If lnpVaria = 0
				llVerLimite = .F.
			Endif
		Else
			lnvVaria = lnValVars
			lnvValo  = lnValos
			lnpVaria = lnVars
		Endif
		If llVerLimite
			Limite = Thisform.Parentform.Chklimite(lnvValo,0,lnvVaria,lnpVaria)
		Endif
	Endif
	If !Limite
		Return .F.
	Endif

	lcPosicao = TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str( TprMvCab.Numes, 6 )

	lcUpDate = [UpDate SigMvCab Set Valos=]+Str(TprMvCab.valos,12,2)+[, Valinis=]+Str(TprMvCab.ValInis,12,2)+[, ]+;
		[ValDevs=]+Str(TprMvCab.ValDevs,12,2)+[, ValServs=]+Str(TprMvCab.ValServs,12,2)+[, ValTrans=]+Str(TprMvCab.ValTrans,12,2)+[, ] + ;
		[ValEncs=]+Str(TprMvCab.ValEncs,12,2)+[, ValVars=]+Str(TprMvCab.ValVars,12,2)+[, ] + ;
		[Vars=]+Str(TprMvCab.Vars,12,2)+[,ValVarPs=]+Str(TprMvCab.ValVarPs,12,2)+[, ]+;
		[Usuals = ']+Usuar+[' Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']

	If Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1
		Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .F.
	Endif

	Select xPar
	Replace All Dopes With TprMvCab.Dopes, ;
		Numes With TprMvCab.Numes
	Replace All DataS With Date() For Empty( DataS )
	Go Top

	llOk = .T.
	*!*		If InList(crSigCdOpe.EdComis,1,3,4)
	*!*			llOk = ThisForm.ParentForm.Comissao(.t.)
	*!*		Else
	*!*			llOk = ThisForm.ParentForm.Comissao()
	*!*		EndIf

	If Not llOk Or Not Thisform.Parentform.Carrega_comi() Or ;
			Not Thisform.Parentform.Carrega_par()
		Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Rollback()
	Else
		Ret = .T.
		*** Apagando a forma de pagamento anterior

		=Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigMvCcr','CrTmpMccr','EmpdopNums',lcPosicao)

*!*			Select CrTmpMccr
*!*			Scan
*!*				=fRecalculaS( CrTmpMccr.Grupos, CrTmpMccr.Contas, CrTmpMccr.DataS, CrTmpMccr.Moedas, Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr )
*!*			Endscan

		Select Distinct EmpDopNcs From CrTmpMccr Into Cursor CrMccr
		Select CrMccr
		Scan
			lcDelete = [Delete From SigOpCoc Where EmpdopNcs = ']+CrMccr.EmpDopNcs+[']
			If (Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,' ') < 1)
				=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigOpCoc)')
				Ret = .F.
			Endif
		Endscan

		lcDelete = [Delete From SigMvCcr Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		lcSql = [Select Distinct EmpDopNcs From SigMvCpv Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And (Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcSql, 'crMccr') < 1)
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrPMccr)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		If Ret
			Select CrMccr
			Scan
				lcDelete = [Delete From SigOpCoc Where EmpdopNcs = ']+CrMccr.EmpDopNcs+[']
				If (Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,' ') < 1)
					=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigOpCoc)')
					Ret = .F.
				Endif
			Endscan
		Endif

		lcDelete = [Delete From SigMvCpv Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigMvCpv)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		lcDelete = [Delete From SigMvPar Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigMvPar)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		lcDelete = [Delete From SigCdCmm Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigCdCmm)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		lcDelete = [Delete From SigChLcq Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigChLcq)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		lcDelete = [Delete From SigCdCrd Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigCdCrd)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		If Ret
			=Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCqChm','CrTmpChm','EmpDopNums',lcPosicao)
			Select CrTmpChm
			Scan
				lcDelete = [Delete From SigChe where Nums = ']+CrTmpChm.Nums+[']
				If Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
					Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigChe)')
					Return .F.
				Endif
			Endscan
		Endif

		lcDelete = [Delete From SigCqChm Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigCqChm)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		lcDelete = [Delete From SigCdCar Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigCdCar)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		lcDelete = [Delete From SigCdCtt Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigCdCtt)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		lcSql = [select EspecieNfs From SigMvNfi where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcSql,'xNfis') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (select SigMvNfi)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		If Ret
			Go Top In xNFis
			If Not Eof([xNfis]) And Not Empty(xNFis.EspecieNfs)
				Update cqSigMvCcr  Set EspecieNfs = xNFis.EspecieNfs
				Update CrSigMvCpv Set EspecieNfs = xNFis.EspecieNfs
			Endif
		Endif

		lpData = Datetime()
		lcSql = [Update SigMvCab Set DtAlts = ?lpdata Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcPosicao+[']
		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcSql,'') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Update SigMvCab)')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		* Inserido Por Richard em 03/04/2012 Para Erros Detectados Por Roberto/Cleber
		lcQuery = [Select Fpags, Contabs ] + ;
			[From SigOpFpo ] + ;
			[Where Dopes = '] + TprMvCab.Dopes + [' And ] + ;
			[Contabs = 1]

		If Ret And Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.sqlexecute(lcQuery,'LocalFpgCtb') < 1
			Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão - LocalFpgCtb')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif

		If Ret
			Select LocalFpgCtb
			Scan
				Update crSigMvPar Set VPags = valos, Pagos = [0] Where Fpags = LocalFpgCtb.Fpags
			Endscan
			Use In LocalFpgCtb
			Go Top In crSigMvPar
		EndIf
		
		* Richard em 02/10/2014 - Incluir o CPROS no cursor usado
		Select a.*, b.cpros ;
			From crSigMvOrp a, crSigMvOrc b ;
			Where a.EmpDopNums = crSigMvCab.EmpDopNums And ;
			a.EmpDopNums = b.EmpDopNums And ;
			a.CItens = b.CItens ;
			Into Cursor fSigOpFin_Orp

		=fSigOpFin_Lancs(Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr, crSigMvCab.Dopes, [crSigMvCab], [crSigMvItn], [crSigMvNfi], [crSigMvPar], [fSigOpFin_Orp])
		* Fim: Richard em 25/03/2014 - Gravar Lançamentos da Configuração Financeira da Movimentação
		******************************************************************************

		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigMvPar')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CqSigMvCcr')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigCdCmm')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigChe')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigCqChm')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigChLcq')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigCdCar')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigCdCtt')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigCdCrd')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigCdLca')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigMvCpv')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And !Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Update('CrSigOpCoc')
			Ret = .F.
		Endif
		If Ret And Not fRecalculaS( .T., Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr )
			Ret = .F.
		EndIf
		
		TransOk = .T.
		If Ret
			Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Commit()
		Else
			Thisform.Parentform.poDataMgr.Rollback()
			TransOk = .F.
		EndIf
		
		lcTransacao = Iif(!TransOk,'Falha na Gravação - ','') + TprMvCab.Emps+' '+Alltrim(TprMvCab.Dopes)+' '+Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)
		lcDopes = TprMvCab.Dopes
		=fGravarLog('T', lcDopes, 'F.PAGTO', lcTransacao, Usuar )
	Endif

	TransOk = .T.

	Select xPar
	Zap
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Zap

Endif

Thisform.Parentform.Enabled = .T.
Thisform.Release

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE verifcheq
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE verifcheq
Parameter wbanco, wagenc, wconta, wnunch
Local _Order, _RecAnt, lcTpErr, sd, Correto
lcTpErr = ''

Declare LaCampo(4,2)

Select xpar
_order  = order()
_recant = recno()
sd = .t.
correto = .t.
banco = wbanco
agencia = wagenc
numero = wconta
conta = wnunch

if correto
	if !fChkDigitos(banco)
		correto = .f.
	endif
endif
if correto
	if !fChkDigitos(agencia)
		correto = .f.
	endif
endif
if correto
	if !fChkDigitos(numero)
		correto = .f.
	endif
endif
if correto
	if !fChkDigitos(conta)
		correto = .f.
	endif
endif
if !correto
	Messagebox('Leitura de cheque incorreta!',0+48,'')
	sd=.f.
endif

If sd
	set order to chq
	if seek( wbanco+wagenc+wconta+wnunch ) and _recant = recno()
		skip
	endif
	if !eof() and wbanco+wagenc+wconta+wnunch = bancos+agencias+contas+numeros
		messagebox('Cheque já lançado !!',0+48,'')
		lcTpErr = 'Cheque já lançado!!'
		sd = .f.
	endif
Endif

if sd
	LaCampo(1,1)='Bancos'
	LaCampo(2,1)='Agencias'
	LaCampo(3,1)='Ncontas'
	LaCampo(4,1)='Ncheques'
	LaCampo(1,2)=wbanco
	LaCampo(2,2)=wagenc
	LaCampo(3,2)=wconta
	LaCampo(4,2)=wnunch
	If ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr.ChkRegister('SigCqChi',@laCampo)
		messagebox('Cheque já cadastrado !!',0+48,'')
		lcTpErr = 'Cheque já cadastrado !!'
		sd = .f.
	Endif
EndIf
select xpar
set order to (_order)
if _recant <> 0
	go _recant
endif
return sd
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Init
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Init
LParameters pForm, datses, plAltDireta, plAutomatico

Local lnLeft, lnTpLeCheques, lnVImpSer

Set Datasession to datses

*** Mena - 26/05/2014 - incluido campo com total de impostos na tela ***
*** mena - 30/06/2014 - verifica se considera o imposto nO RECEBIMENTO ***
lcConta = crSigMvNfi.CliFors
lnVImpSer = 0

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [ISS], pForm.podatamgr )
lnVImpSer = lnVImpSer + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vtotiss, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [INSS], pForm.podatamgr )
lnVImpSer = lnVImpSer + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vinss, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [IRRF], pForm.podatamgr )
lnVImpSer = lnVImpSer + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.virf, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [CSSL], pForm.podatamgr )
lnVImpSer = lnVImpSer + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vcsll, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [PIS], pForm.podatamgr )
lnVImpSer = lnVImpSer + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vpis, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [COFINS], pForm.podatamgr)
lnVImpSer = lnVImpSer + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vcofins, 0)

&&lnVImpSer = (crSigmvnfi.vcofins + crSigmvnfi.vcsll + crSigmvnfi.vinss + crSigmvnfi.virf + crSigmvnfi.vpis + crSigmvnfi.vtotiss)	&& mena 03/06/2014 valiis)
lnVImpSer = IIF(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 27, 1)))=2,-lnVImpSer,lnVImpSer) 

With Thisform
	.ParentForm = pForm
	.AltDireta  = plAltDireta
	.Op_Escolha = pForm.pcEscolha
	.Ok.Cancel = !plAltDireta
	.ReadComm.Enabled = .F.

	.SairAuto = plAutomatico

	.DescnPar = CrSigOpCdd.VarPs = 1
	.Parentform.validadesconto = ( CrSigCdOpe.Vars <> 3 )
	.ParcDesconto = 0
	.Bcds = 0
	.BinCartao = ''
	.AplicouTabd = .f.

	.Desconto.Get_PorcVariação.InputMask  = [9999] + Iif(CrSigOpCdd.EditAgends > 0, [.] + Replicate([9], CrSigOpCdd.EditAgends), [])
	.Acrescimo.Get_PorcVariação.InputMask = [9999] + Iif(CrSigOpCdd.EditAgends > 0, [.] + Replicate([9], CrSigOpCdd.EditAgends), [])
	.Get_vdescP.Visible = CrSigOpCdd.VarPs = 1
	.Get_pdescP.Visible = CrSigOpCdd.VarPs = 1

	If Type([crSigOpCdi.chkOpnCsh])=[N] And InList(crSigOpCdi.chkOpnCsh,1,4)
		
		Thisform.btnRetCash.Visible = .T.
		
		If crSigOpCdi.chkOpnCsh = 1
			ThisForm.getValUtiCsh.Visible = .T.
			ThisForm.lblValUtiCsh.Visible = .T.
			ThisForm.btnCash.Visible = .T.
		EndIf 
		
		If Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR', 'CONSULTAR' ) 
			lcValorCash=ThisForm.parentform.OpenCash(TprMvCab.Contads)	
			ThisForm.lbl_Mensagem.Visible = .T.	
			ThisForm.lbl_Mensagem.Caption = [Saldo de Bônus: ] + Alltrim(lcValorCash)
			ThisForm.lbl_Mensagem.Refresh
		EndIf 
		
		If Used([crSigmvItd])
			Select crSigmvItd 
			Go Top
			Scan 
				If Not IsEmpty(crSigmvItd.TpDesc)
					If Alltrim(crSigMvItd.cdesc) = '40CASHBACK'
						llPermiteDesc = .F.
						ThisForm.BlqPossuiBonus = .T.
					Else
						ThisForm.BlqPossuiBonus = .F.
					EndIf 
				EndIf

			EndScan  
		EndIf 
		
		If !llPermiteDesc
			ThisForm.getValUtiCsh.Value = 0 
			ThisForm.getValUtiCsh.Enabled = .F.
			ThisForm.btnCash.Enabled = .F.
		EndIf 
	EndIf

	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crSigCdEmp)')

									Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) )

Go Top

cotoper = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( Thisform.Get_moeoper.Value ) 

ctotal = 0
Select xPar
Scan
	=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
	cotacao = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( xPar.moefpgs ) 
	lnInd   = Iif(CrSigOpFp.Indices=0,1,CrSigOpFp.Indices)
	If CrSigOpFp.Trocos = 1
		ctotal = Round( ctotal - ( xPar.valos * (xPar.cotfpgs*lnInd) / cotoper ), 2 )
	Else
		ctotal = Round( ctotal + ( xPar.valos * (xPar.cotfpgs*lnInd) / cotoper ), 2 )
	Endif
Endscan
*** Mena - 26/05/2014 - soma o valor dos impostos de serviço no recebimento ***
*** mena - 30/06/2014 - verifica se considera o imposto nO RECEBIMENTO ***
lcConta = crSigMvNfi.CliFors

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [ISS], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vtotiss, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [INSS], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vinss, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [IRRF], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.virf, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [CSSL], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vcsll, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [PIS], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr )
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vpis, 0)

lnFI = FSigOpFin_NF(lcConta, [], [COFINS], ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr)
cTotal= cTotal + IIF(lnFI = 1,crSigmvnfi.vcofins, 0)

&&cTotal = cTotal + (crSigmvnfi.vcofins + crSigmvnfi.vcsll + crSigmvnfi.vinss + crSigmvnfi.virf + crSigmvnfi.vpis + crSigmvnfi.vtotiss)	&& mena 03/06/2014 valiis)

**** Configura Leitora de Cheques
If Not fLerSettings('CrSigSyCom',ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr)
	Return 0
EndIf
Select * From crSigSyCom Where Tipos = 'Leitora Cheques' Into Cursor CrCfcom ReadWrite
Select CrCfCom
Go Top

TpLeChq=TpLeChqs

	TpLeChq='T'
Endif
Thisform.TipoLeitora=TpLeChq
Thisform.LeituraSerial = ''
With Thisform
	.AvisoPortaT.Visible = (TpLeChq='T')
	.AvisoPortaC.Visible = (TpLeChq='C')
	.AvisoPortaA.Visible = (TpLeChq='M')
	.AvisoPortaM.Visible = .f.
	.AvisoPortaS.Visible = (TpLeChq='S')
	.AvisoPortaK.Visible = (TpLeChq='K')
EndWith
 
Do Case
	Case TpLeChq='S'
		Thisform.ReadComm.Configura(crCfCom.Portas,crCfCom.Dtrs,crCfCom.Rtss,;
									crCfCom.Hands,crCfCom.Settings,crCfCom.InBufs,crCfCom.OutBufs,;
									crCfCom.InModes,crCfCom.InLens)
		If Thisform.ReadComm.PortaError
			Thisform.AvisoPortaM.Visible = .t.
			Thisform.AvisoPortaS.Visible = .f.
		Endif
EndCase

ThisForm.ItSemBonus = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr, CrSigCdOpe.Dopes, .f., .t. )

lnVars  = TprMvCab.Vars
lnvVars = TprMvCab.ValVars

If CrSigOpCdd.VarPs = 1
	lnvVars = TprMvCab.ValVars - TprMvCab.ValVarPs
	If Abs(TprMvCab.ValVarPs) > Abs(TprMvCab.ValVars)
		lnvVars = Abs(lnvVars)
	Endif
	xServ  = Iif(CrSigMvNfi.vTotSers <> 0,CrSigMvNfi.vTotSers,TprMvCab.ValServs)
	_Total = Iif(CrSigCdOpe.Vars = 4,ThisForm.ItSemBonus-TprMvCab.ValDevs,TprMvCab.Valinis) + xServ
	lnVars = IIf(_Total=0, 0, (lnvVars / _Total) * 100)
Endif

Do Case
	Case lnVars < 0 Or (ThisForm.AplicaVariacao And lnVars = 0 )
		Thisform.Desconto.Get_PorcVariação.value = Round(Abs(lnVars), CrSigOpCdd.EditAgends)
		Thisform.Desconto.Get_TotVariação.value  = Abs(lnvVars)
		Thisform.Acrescimo.Enabled=.f.
		Thisform.Desconto.Enabled=.t.
	Case lnVars > 0
		Thisform.Acrescimo.Get_PorcVariação.value = Round(lnVars, CrSigOpCdd.EditAgends)
		Thisform.Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.value  = lnvVars
		Thisform.Acrescimo.Enabled=.t.
		Thisform.Desconto.Enabled=.f.
	Case TprMvCab.Vars = 0
		Thisform.Acrescimo.Enabled=.t.
		Thisform.Desconto.Enabled=.t.
EndCase

With Thisform
	.CalculaDesconto(0,.t.)
	.Get_Valtot.Value = ThisForm.Calculasaldo()

	.Get_TotForma.Value = ctotal
	.Get_Saldo.Value    = .Get_ValTot.Value - .Get_TotForma.Value
	.Get_Fpg.Value = ''
	.Get_NPar.Value = 0
	.Get_VPar.Value = 0
	.Dataget_venc.Value = {}
	.Get_Cotacao.Value = 0
	
	If (CrSigOpCdi.varsdesacr<>2)
		.Desconto.Visible = ( InList(crSigCdOpe.Vars,1,3,4,5,6) And CrSigCdOpe.ParAutos <> 5 And (TprMvCab.ValInis <> 0 Or TprMvCab.valServs <> 0 ) )
	EndIf 
	If (CrSigOpCdi.varsdesacr<>1)
		.Acrescimo.Visible = ( InList(crSigCdOpe.Vars,1,4,5,6) And CrSigCdOpe.ParAutos <> 5 And (TprMvCab.ValInis <> 0 Or TprMvCab.valServs <> 0 ) )
	EndIf 	
	.Refresh
	
EndWith

If CrSigOpCdc.HabServs = 1
	ThisForm.say21.Visible = .f.
	ThisForm.Get_TotServiço.Visible = .f.
Endif

Select xPar
Go Top
If Eof() And crSigCdOpe.LPrecos # 3 And !Empty( TprMvCab.LPrecos ) 
	=ThisForm.Parentform.PoDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdLpc','CrSigCdLpc','LPrecos',TprMvCab.LPrecos)
	This.Get_Fpg.Value = crSigCdLpc.FPags
Endif

ThisForm.Apaga.Enabled = Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And CrSigCdOpe.ParAutos <> 5

	Return .f.

	Endif

		m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute('select codigos,descrs,codpai,visuals from SigFiCt order by 1','crSigFiCT')
	EndIf 
	If Used('crSigFiCT')
		Select ('crSigFiCT')
		Index On codigos Tag codigos
		Set Order To ('') In ('crSigFiCT')
	EndIf

ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.cursorquery('SigCdMoe','LocalMoe',.f.,.f.)

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getbandeira
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 6
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
*** mena - 19/05/2017 - utilizado no relatorio de fechamento da venda diária  ***
If !Empty(This.Value)
	Thisform.parentform.podatamgr.SqlExecute([Select * from SigCdCrt ],"crBan")
	Select CrBan
	Index On descs Tag descs
	Set Near On
	If Not Seek(This.Value)
		xBrow=Createobject('fwbuscaint','crBan','descs',This.Value,'Seleção',.T.)
		xBrow.mAddColuna('DESCS','','Bandeira',.T.)
		xBrow.Show
	Endif
	This.Value = CrBan.descs
	This.Refresh
Else
	This.Value = ''
	This.Refresh
Endif
Return(.T.)

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.Processar
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 10
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Thisform.mGetcsSigFpag(.T.)

Select csSigOpFp
Locate for .F.
Go Top
If Reccount([csSigOpFp]) = 0
	This.parent.Visible = .F.
	This.parent.parent.Visible = .F.
EndIf 

Select csSigOpFp
Go Top
If Reccount([csSigOpFp])=1
	ThisForm.Get_fpg.Value = csSigOpFp.FPags
	ThisForm.Get_fpg.Valid()
	ThisForm.Get_fpg.Refresh
	ThisForm.Get_fpg.LostFocus()
	
	ThisForm.Get_npar.SetFocus()
	
Else
	If !Seek( Replicate(Chr(254),10), 'csSigOpFp', 'FPags' )
		Go Top
		loBrowse = CreateObject( 'fwbuscasel', 'csSigOpFp', 'FPags', Space(10), 'Cadastro de Forma de Pagamento' ) &&Replicate(Chr(254),10)
		loBrowse.AddColuna( 'FPags', '', 'Código', .t., 'FPags' )
		loBrowse.AddColuna( 'Descrs', '', 'Descrição' )
		loBrowse.AddColuna( 'MoeFPgs', 'XXXXX', 'Moeda' )
		loBrowse.AddColuna( 'FParcs', '99', ' #' )
		loBrowse.AddColuna( 'bandeira', '', 'Bandeira' )
		loBrowse.Show
		ThisForm.Get_fpg.Value = Iif( Lastkey() == 27, '', csSigOpFp.FPags )
		If !Empty(ThisForm.Get_fpg.Value)
			=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',ThisForm.Get_fpg.Value)
		EndIf
		ThisForm.Get_fpg.Valid()
		ThisForm.Get_fpg.Refresh
		ThisForm.Get_fpg.LostFocus()
		
		ThisForm.Get_npar.SetFocus()	
	EndIf
EndIf 

This.parent.getFormas.Visible = .F.
This.parent.getAdquirente.Visible = .F.
This.parent.getParcela.Visible = .F.
This.Parent.getbandeira.Visible = .F.
This.parent.Say2.Visible = .F.
This.parent.Say3.Visible = .F.
This.parent.Say4.Visible = .F.
This.parent.Say6.Visible = .F.
	
This.parent.Visible = .F.
This.parent.parent.Visible = .F.

If Not Empty(This.parent.getParcela.Value) And Reccount([csSigOpFp])>=1
	ThisForm.Get_npar.Value = This.parent.getParcela.Value
	ThisForm.Get_npar.Refresh
EndIf 

This.parent.getFormas.Value = []
This.parent.getAdquirente.Value = []
This.Parent.getbandeira.Value = []
This.parent.getParcela.Value = 0
	
This.parent.getFormas.Refresh
This.parent.getAdquirente.Refresh
This.Parent.getbandeira.Refresh
This.parent.getParcela.Refresh
This.parent.Say2.Refresh
This.parent.Say3.Refresh
This.parent.Say4.Refresh
This.parent.Say6.Refresh

This.parent.Refresh 
ENDPROC

* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.cntFundo.cntMascPgto.getModalidade

* Registro: 20
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
*** mena - 02/03/2016 - este campo foi criado para ser utilizado no recebimento da operação ***
Local lnRec, lcFpags
If !Empty(This.Value)
	Thisform.parentform.podatamgr.sqlexecute([Select * from SigCdMod],"crMod")
	Select crMod
	Index On Descs Tag Descs 
	Set Near On
	If Not Seek(This.Value)
		xBrow=Createobject('fwbuscaint','crMod','Descs',This.Value,'Seleção',.T.)
		xBrow.mAddColuna('Descs','','Descrição',.T.)
		xBrow.Show
	Endif
	This.Value = crMod.Descs
	This.Refresh
Else
	This.Value = ''
	This.Refresh
Endif


If Not Empty(This.Value)
	This.parent.getFormas.Visible = Iif(Alltrim(Upper(This.Value)) = 'CARTÃO',.T.,.F.)
	This.parent.getAdquirente.Visible = Iif(Alltrim(Upper(This.Value)) = 'CARTÃO',.T.,.F.)
	This.Parent.getbandeira.Visible = Iif(Alltrim(Upper(This.Value)) = 'CARTÃO',.T.,.F.)
	This.parent.getParcela.Visible = Iif(Alltrim(Upper(This.Value)) = 'CARTÃO',.T.,.F.)
	This.parent.Say2.Visible = Iif(Alltrim(Upper(This.Value)) = 'CARTÃO',.T.,.F.)
	This.parent.Say3.Visible = Iif(Alltrim(Upper(This.Value)) = 'CARTÃO',.T.,.F.)
	This.parent.Say4.Visible = Iif(Alltrim(Upper(This.Value)) = 'CARTÃO',.T.,.F.)
	This.parent.Say6.Visible = Iif(Alltrim(Upper(This.Value)) = 'CARTÃO',.T.,.F.)
	
	This.parent.getFormas.Refresh
	This.parent.getAdquirente.Refresh
	This.Parent.getbandeira.Refresh
	This.parent.getParcela.Refresh
	This.parent.Say2.Refresh
	This.parent.Say3.Refresh
	This.parent.Say4.Refresh
	This.parent.Say6.Refresh
	
EndIf 


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
This.parent.getFormas.Value = ''
This.parent.getAdquirente.Value = ''
This.Parent.getbandeira.Value = ''
This.parent.getParcela.Value = 0
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.CntCheque
* Classe: container (Base: container)
* Registro: 24
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lcCheque, lcFoco, lcMsg, llLimpa, lnChqOpers, lcOperAutos, lcNums, llAprova
Local lcBanco, lcAgencia, lcConta, lcNumero, lnRecNo, lnNumero, lcAprovacao, lnAlineas

Go Top In crSigCdPam
Thisform.GetProts = .F.
Thisform.CntCheque.Get_ProtChq.Value = ''
Keyboard '{DNARROW}'
=Inkey(0)

If InList(Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And Not Thisform.NOkLostChe
	Select xPar
	=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
	lcCheque = xPar.Bancos + xPar.Agencias + xPar.Contas + xPar.Numeros
	lcFoco   = Iif( crSigCdPam.nCChequePs = 1, 'ConsultaChq','get_fpg') &&Grade.Valor.Text1' ) &&get_fpg')
	lcMsg    = ''
	llLimpa  = .F.
	lnChqOpers = Iif( (crSigCdOpe.COpers = 1 And CrSigOpFp.Trocos = 1) Or ;
					  (crSigCdOpe.COpers = 2 And CrSigOpFp.Trocos # 1), 2, 1)

	If Not Empty(lcCheque) And Not xPar.InsChs
		If lnChqOpers = 1
			llLimpa = !fChkCheques( xPar.Bancos, xPar.Agencias, xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros,Thisform.parentform.podatamgr, .f., xPar.Dopes)
		Else
			Declare LaCampo(4,2)
			LaCampo(1,1)='Bancos'
			LaCampo(2,1)='Agencias'
			LaCampo(3,1)='nContas'
			LaCampo(4,1)='Ncheques'
			LaCampo(1,2)=xPar.Bancos
			LaCampo(2,2)=xPar.Agencias
			LaCampo(3,2)=xPar.Contas
			LaCampo(4,2)=xPar.Numeros
			If !ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque',@LaCampo)
				lcMsg     = 'Cheque Não Está Cadastrado.'
				lcCaption = 'Cheque Com Dados Incorretos'
				llLimpa   = .T.
			Else

				Select [ ] as Agrupar,Count(1) as nTotal From xPar ;
					Where Not Empty(Bancos + Agencias + Contas + Numeros) And ;
						  Bancos + Agencias + Contas + Numeros = lcCheque ;
					Into Cursor csVerChq Group by 1
				If csVerChq.nTotal > 1
					lcMsg     = 'Cheque já Lançado Nessa Movimentação'
					lcCaption = 'Cheque já Lançado'
					llLimpa   = .T.
				Else
					=ThisForm.ParentForm.PoDatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpt','CrSigCdOpt','Operacaos',CrCheque.Umovs)
					If crSigCdOpt.Tipos # 'DB'
						lcMsg     = 'Cheque Não Pode Ser Utilizado Nessa Movimentação.'
						lcCaption = 'Última Operação do Cheque: ' + AllTrim(crCheque.UMovs)
						llLimpa   = .T.
					Else
						lcUpDate = [Update SigChe Set Valors = Valors where Nums = ']+Crcheque.Nums+[']
						If Thisform.Parentform.Podatamgr.sqlexecute(lcUpdate,'') < 1
							lcMsg     = 'Cheque Sendo Utilizado por Outro Usuário.'
							lcCaption = 'Movimentação do Cheque Não Permitida' + AllTrim(crCheque.UMovs)
							llLimpa   = .T.
							lcFoco    = 'CntCheque.Get_Bco'
						Else
							Select xPar
							lnRecNo    = Recno()
							lcNums     = crCheque.Nums
							lnNAlineas = crCheque.NAlineas
							Replace xPar.InsChs  With .T., ;
									xPar.NumChqs With lcNums, ;
									xPar.Valos   With crCheque.Valors, ;
									xPar.Vencs   With crCheque.Vencs
							lcFoco = 'Get_Fpg'
							Select xPar
							=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
							lcOperAutos = ''
							If Not Empty(CrSigOpFp.OperAutos)
								If Not Empty(lnNAlineas) And ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdAli','CrSigCdAli','Codigos',lnNAlineas)
									lcOperAutos = crSigCdAli.FPagSAutos
								Else
									lcOperAutos = CrSigOpFp.OperAutos
								EndIf
							EndIf
							
							If Not Empty(lcOperAutos) And ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',lcOperAutos)
								Select xPar
								Scatter MemVar Memo
								m.Fpags      = lcOperAutos
								m.NumChqs    = lcNums
								m.Bancos     = ''
								m.Agencias   = ''
								m.Contas     = ''
								m.Numeros    = ''
								m.adquirente = ''
								m.autoriza   = ''
								m.nsu 		 = ''
								m.Vencs      = TprMvCab.Datas
								Insert Into xPar From MemVar
							Endif
							Thisform.mTotalForma()								
							Select xPar
							Goto (lnRecNo)
							Thisform.Grade.Refresh
						EndIf
					EndIf
				EndIf
			EndIf	
		Endif
	Endif

	If Not Empty(lcMsg)
		MessageBox( lcMsg, 48, lcCaption )
	Endif

	If llLimpa
		Thisform.Limpa_Che()
		lcFoco = Iif(lnChqOpers = 2, 'CntCheque.Get_ProtChq', 'CntCheque.Get_Bco')
	Else
		=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
		llAprova = ( CrSigOpFp.Aprovas = 1 )
		*!& 21/11/2016 If lnChqOpers = 1 And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs) And Not Empty(xPar.Numeros) &&And crCfCom.TpLeChqs = 'M' 
		If lnChqOpers = 1 And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF' And Not Empty(xPar.Numeros) &&And crCfCom.TpLeChqs = 'M' 
			lcBanco   = xPar.Bancos
			lcAgencia = xPar.Agencias
			lcConta   = xPar.Contas
			lcNumero  = xPar.Numeros
			lnRecNo   = RecNo('xPar')
			Select xPar
			Scan
				=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
				If CrSigOpFp.Infos='C' And Empty( AllTrim(xPar.Bancos) + AllTrim(xPar.Agencias) + ;
						    AllTrim(xPar.Contas) + AllTrim(xPar.Numeros) )
					lnNumero = Val(lcNumero) + 1
					lcNumero = Transform(lnNumero,'@L 999999')
					Select xPar
					Replace Bancos   With lcBanco,   ;
							Agencias With lcAgencia, ;
							Contas   With lcConta,   ;
							Numeros  With lcNumero
				Endif
			EndScan
			Goto (lnRecno)
		EndIf
		
		If llAprova And Thisform.CntCheque.Get_Aprovacao.Visible And ;
				Not Empty(Thisform.CntCheque.Get_Aprovacao.Value)
			lcAprovacao = Thisform.CntCheque.Get_Aprovacao.Value					
			Select xPar
			lnRecNo = RecNo('xPar')
			Replace All xPar.NoCreditos With lcAprovacao For xPar.NumOlds < 0
			Replace All xPar.NumOlds With 0 For xPar.NumOlds < 0
			Select xPar
			Goto (lnRecno)
		EndIf
	EndIf

	ThisForm.&lcFoco..SetFocus
Endif
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.CntCheque.Get_Aprovacao
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 25
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return( InList(Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And Not xPar.InsChs )

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.CntCheque.Get_num
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 27
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
*	If CrSigOpFp.TpCheques = 3
*		This.Value = PadL(AllTrim(This.Value),6,'0')
*	Endif
	This.Value = PadL(AllTrim(This.Value),6,'0') && Julian 13/07/2017 - Removi a validação do TpCheques = 3 pois esta condição não está sendo atendida
	If Not fChkDigitos(xPar.Numeros) 
		MessageBox('Número Inválido !',0+48,'')
		This.Value =''
		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local llHabilita

Do Case
	*** mena - 21/11/2016 - cCupomNvs =TEF ou <> TEF ***Case CrCfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case CrCfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(xPar.nIdCheps)
		
	*** mena - 21/11/2016 - cCupomNvs =TEF ou <> TEF ***Case CrCfCom.TpLeChqs = 'M' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case CrCfCom.TpLeChqs = 'M' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(xPar.nIdCheps)

	*** mena - 21/11/2016 - cCupomNvs =TEF ou <> TEF ***Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'S' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'S' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(This.Value) Or ;
					 (Not Empty(This.Value) And Empty(xPar.nidcheps) And CrSigOpFp.TpCheques = 3)

	*** mena - 21/11/2016 - cCupomNvs =TEF ou <> TEF ***Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'T' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'T' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

	*** mena - 21/11/2016 - cCupomNvs =TEF ou <> TEF ***Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'K' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'K' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

	OtherWise
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

EndCase

Return( Inlist( Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR' ) And ;
		Not ( crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 ) And llHabilita And Not xPar.InsChs )

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.CntCheque.Get_conta
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 28
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcConta, lcNumero

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
*!*	If crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	If crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		If Len( This.Value ) => 6 
			lcConta = PadL( Substr( This.Value, 1, 6), 10, '0' )
			If Len( This.Value ) > 6
				lcNumero = Substr( This.Value, 7, 6 )
			Else
				lcNumero = ''
			Endif
			Select xPar
			Replace xPar.Contas  With lcConta, ;
					xPar.Numeros With lcNumero
				
			With Thisform.CntCheque
				.Get_Conta.Value = lcConta
				.Get_Num.Value   = AllTrim(lcNumero)
				.Refresh
			Endwith
		EndIf

		If CrSigOpFp.TpCheques = 3
			This.Value = PadL(AllTrim(This.Value),10,'0')
		Endif
		If Not fChkDigitos(PadL(AllTrim(xPar.Contas),10,'0')) 
			MessageBox('Conta Inválida !',0+48,'')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
	ELSE
*		Julian 13/07/2017
*		Condição do TpCheques = 3 não está sendo atendida
*		If CrSigOpFp.TpCheques = 3
*			This.Value = PadL(AllTrim(This.Value),10,'0')
*		Endif
		This.Value = PADL(ALLTRIM(This.Value), 10, '0')
		If Not fChkDigitos(xPar.Contas) 
			MessageBox('Conta Inválida !',0+48,'')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local llHabilita

Do Case
	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(xPar.nIdCheps)
		
	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'M' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'M' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(xPar.nIdCheps)

	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'S' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'S' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(This.Value) Or ;
					 (Not Empty(This.Value) And Empty(xPar.nidcheps) And CrSigOpFp.TpCheques = 3)

	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'T' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'T' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'K' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'K' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

	OtherWise
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

EndCase

Return( Inlist( Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR' ) And ;
		Not ( crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 ) And llHabilita And Not xPar.InsChs )
ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE Valid

PROCEDURE Valid
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
	If CrSigOpFp.TpCheques = 3
		This.Value = PadL(AllTrim(This.Value),4,'0')
	Endif

		MessageBox('Agencia Inválida !',0+48,'')

		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	If Not fChkDigitos(xPar.Agencias) 
		MessageBox('Agencia Inválida !',0+48,'')
		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

PROCEDURE When
Local llHabilita

Do Case
	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(xPar.nIdCheps)
		
	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'M' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'M' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(xPar.nIdCheps)

	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'S' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'S' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(This.Value) Or ;
					 (Not Empty(This.Value) And Empty(xPar.nidcheps) And CrSigOpFp.TpCheques = 3)
	
	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'T' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'T' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'K' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'K' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

	OtherWise
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

EndCase

Return( Inlist( Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR' ) And ;
		Not (crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1) And llHabilita And Not xPar.InsChs )
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.CntCheque.Get_bco
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 34
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus

Thisform.ReadComm.Enabled = .F.

If LastKey()=9
	Thisform.Grade.valor.text1.SetFocus
Else
	If Thisform.Leitor
		If Not Empty(xPar.Bancos)
			If Not fChkCheques(xPar.Bancos,xPar.Agencias,xPar.Contas,xPar.Numeros,thisform.parentform.podatamgr,.f.,xPar.Dopes)
				Replace bancos With '', agencias With '', contas With '', numeros With ''
				With Thisform.CntCheque
					.Get_Bco.Value = ''
					.Get_Age.Value = ''
					.Get_Conta.Value = ''
					.Get_Num.Value = ''
				EndWith
				This.Value = ''
				Thisform.CntCheque.Get_Num.SetFocus	
			Else
				Thisform.CntCheque.Get_Num.SetFocus	
			Endif
		Endif
	Else
		Thisform.CntCheque.Get_Age.SetFocus
	Endif
Endif

Thisform.Leitor = .F.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local Leitura, Tecla, Contador, lcMsg, lcCaption, lcBanco, lcAgencia, lcConta, lcNumero
Local lnLeTeclado

Goto Top In crSigCdPam
Leitura     = ''
lcMsg       = ''
lnLeTeclado = Iif( crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C', 19, 33)
Select xPar
Set Order to

If crcfcom.TpLeChqs # 'M' and Lastkey() <> 13 and LastKey() <> 27 And Lastkey() <> 05 And (Empty(This.Value) or crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'U')
	If Not Thisform.Leitor
		If Thisform.TipoLeitora = 'S'
			If Empty(Thisform.LeituraSerial)
				If !Thisform.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen And !Thisform.ReadComm.PortaError
					Thisform.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen = .t.
				EndIf
				Thisform.ReadComm.Enabled = Thisform.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen
				Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !' nowait
			Else
				Leitura = Thisform.LeituraSerial
				Thisform.LeituraSerial = ''
			Endif
		Else
			If Thisform.TipoLeitora = 'K'
				Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !!!' nowait
				llRetorno = PertoDesabilita(Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
				Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !' nowait				
				If Not Empty(crCfCom.Portas)
					llRet = PertoHabilita(crCfCom.Portas,Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					If llRet = 1
						Leitura = PertoAlinha(Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
						If Type('Leitura') = 'C'
							Leitura = Substr(Leitura,5)
						Else
							Leitura = ''
						Endif
					EndIf
				EndIf
				If ! Empty(Leitura)
					llRetorno = PertoExpulsa(Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					llRetorno = PertoDesabilita(Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
				Endif
				Wait Clear
			Else				
				If Thisform.TipoLeitora = 'H'
					Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !!!' nowait
					llRetorno = ChronoDesabilita(crCfCom.Portas)
					Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !' nowait				
					If Not Empty(crCfCom.Portas)
						llRet = ChronoHabilita(crCfCom.Portas)
						If llRet
							Leitura = ChronoAlinhaCMC7(crCfCom.Portas)
							If ! Type('Leitura') = 'C'
								Leitura = ''
							Endif
						EndIf
					EndIf
					If ! Empty(Leitura)
*						llRetorno = ChronoExpulsa(crCfCom.Portas)
						llRetorno = ChronoDesabilita(crCfCom.Portas)
					Endif
					Wait Clear
				Else
					Leitura=ALLTRIM(This.Value)
					Contador = 0
					Do While LastKey()<>27 And Len(Leitura) < lnLeTeclado And ;
							LastKey()<>13 And LastKey()<>03 And ;
							LastKey()<>05 And LastKey()<>18 And ;
							LastKey()<>24 And LastKey()<>148
						tecla = LastKey()
						If Tecla > 0
							Contador = Contador + 1
							Read
							If Contador > 1
								Leitura = Leitura + chr(tecla)
							EndIf
						EndIf
					EndDo
				EndIf
			EndIf
		Endif
		ThisForm.pcCMC7=IIF(LEN(ALLTRIM(Leitura)) >= 33,Leitura,"")
		If len (Leitura)>= lnLeTeclado
			If crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C'
				This.Value = Substr(Leitura,1,3)
				lcBanco    = Substr(Leitura,1,3)
				lcAgencia  = Substr(Leitura,4,4)
				lcConta    = PadL(Substr(Leitura,8,6),10,'0')
				lcNumero   = Substr(Leitura,14,6)
			Else
				If crCfCom.TpLeChqs = 'K'
					This.Value = Substr(Leitura,1,3)
					lcBanco    = Substr(Leitura,1,3)
					lcAgencia  = Substr(Leitura,4,4)
					lcConta    = Substr(Leitura,22,10)
					lcNumero   = Substr(Leitura,13,6)
				Else
					This.Value = Substr(Leitura,2,3)
					lcBanco    = Substr(Leitura,2,3)
					lcAgencia  = Substr(Leitura,5,4)
					lcConta    = Substr(Leitura,23,10)
					lcNumero   = Substr(Leitura,14,6)
				Endif
			EndIf
			Select xPar
			Replace xPar.Bancos   With lcBanco,    ;
					xPar.Agencias With lcAgencia,  ;
					xPar.Contas   With lcConta ;
					xPar.Numeros  With lcNumero
			With Thisform.CntCheque
				.Get_Bco.Refresh
				.Get_Age.Refresh
				.Get_Conta.Refresh
				.Get_Num.Refresh
			EndWith
			Thisform.Leitor = .T.
		Else
			Thisform.Leitor = .F. 	
		Endif
	EndIf
EndIf

lcMsg = Iif( Not Empty(This.Value) And Not fChkDigitos(xPar.Bancos), ;
		'Banco Inválido !','')

If Not Empty(lcMsg)
	MessageBox( lcMsg, 0+48, '')
	Return 0
Else
	Select xPar
	Set Order To
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local llHabilita

llHabilita = .F.
Select crSigCdPam
Goto Top
Do Case
	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'C' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(xPar.nIdCheps)
		
	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'M' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'M' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts And Empty(xPar.nIdCheps)

	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'S' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'S' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'T' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'T' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

	*!* 21/11/2016 Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'K' And Empty(CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
	Case crcfcom.TpLeChqs = 'K' And CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF'
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

	OtherWise
		llHabilita = Not Thisform.GetProts

EndCase

If InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And ;
		llHabilita And Not xPar.InsChs
	If Thisform.TipoLeitora = 'S' 
		IF !Thisform.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen And !Thisform.ReadComm.PortaError
				Thisform.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen = .t.
		EndIf
		Thisform.ReadComm.Enabled = Thisform.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen
		Wait Window Proper('Passe o cheque pela leitora !') NoWait
	Endif
	Return(.T.)
Else
	Return(.F.)
Endif

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.CntCheque.Get_ProtChq
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 35
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcProtocolos, lcCheque

ThisForm.GetProts = .F.
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	lcProtocolos = PadL(AllTrim(This.Value),8,'0')
	If ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCqChm','CrTmpChm','Protocolos',lcProtocolos) And ;
		ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigChe','CrTmpChe','Nums',CrTmpChm.Nums)
		lcCheque = CrTmpChe.Bancos + CrTmpChe.Agencias + CrTmpChe.NContas + CrTmpChe.NCheques
		Select [ ] as Agrupar,Count(1) as nTotal From xPar ;
			Where Not Empty(Bancos + Agencias + Contas + Numeros) And ;
				  Bancos + Agencias + Contas + Numeros = lcCheque ;
			Into Cursor csVerChq Group by 1
		If csVerChq.nTotal # 0
			Messagebox('Cheque: ' + CrTmpChe.Bancos + '-' + CrTmpChe.Agencias + '-' + ;
					   crtmpChe.NContas + '-' + crtmpChe.NCheques + ' já lançado nessa ' + ;
					   'Movimentação ... ',48,'Cheque já Lançado')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0					 
		Endif			
		Select xPar
		Replace xPar.Bancos   With CrTmpChe.Bancos,   ;
				xPar.Agencias With CrTmpChe.Agencias, ;
				xPar.Contas   With CrTmpChe.NContas,  ;
				xPar.Numeros  With CrTmpChe.NCheques, ;
				xPar.Outros   With CrTmpChe.Protocolos
		With Thisform.CntCheque
			.Get_Bco.Refresh
			.Get_Age.Refresh
			.Get_Conta.Refresh
			.Get_Num.Refresh
		EndWith
		Thisform.GetProts = .T.
	Else
		MessageBox('Protocolo ' + lcProtocolos + ' Não Encontrado ... ',48,'')
		This.Value = ''
		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local lnChqOpers

This.peantvalue = This.Value
=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
lnChqOpers = Iif( (crSigCdOpe.COpers = 1 And CrSigOpFp.Trocos = 1) Or ;
				  (crSigCdOpe.COpers = 2 And CrSigOpFp.Trocos # 1), 2, 1)

Return ( InList(Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And (Not Thisform.GetProts) And ;
		 crSigCdOpe.Caixas = 1 And lnChqOpers = 2 And ;
		 Empty(xPar.Bancos + xPar.Agencias + xPar.Contas + xPar.Numeros ) )
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPAG.CntCCreditoS.Get_Protocs
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 44
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If Thisform.GetProts
	Thisform.Get_Fpg.SetFocus
Else
	Thisform.grade.valor.text1.SetFocus
Endif
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcProts, lnRecNo

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	lcProts = PadL( AllTrim(This.Value), 8, '0' )
	
	If ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdCtt','CrTmpCart','CartProts',lcProts)
		lnRecNo = RecNo('xPar')
		Select RecNo() As nRecNo, EmpOs, NCarts From xPar ;
			Where Recno() # lnRecNo And EmpOs = CrTmpCart.Emps And NCarts = CrtmpCart.NCarts ;
			Into Cursor csVerCart
		
		If RecCount('csVerCart') # 0
			MessageBox('Número de Protocolo de Cartão de Crédito já Digitado Nessa Operação.',48,'')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		Else
			Thisform.GetProts = .T.
			Select xPar
			Replace xPar.Outros With lcProts,        ;
					xPar.EmpOs  With CrTmpCart.Emps,   ;
					xPar.NCarts With CrTmpCart.NCarts, ;
					xPar.Valos  With CrTmpCart.Valos,  ;
					xPar.Vencs  With Iif(Not Empty(CrTmpCart.Vencs), CrTmpCart.Vencs, TprMvCab.Datas),  ;
					xPar.InsChs With .T.
			This.Value = ''
			Thisform.CntCCreditoS.Get_Obs.Refresh
		EndIf
	Else
		MessageBox('Número de Protocolo de Cartão de Crédito Inválido.',48,'')
		This.Value = ''
		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return( InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And Not xPar.InsChs And Not Thisform.GetProts )

ENDPROC
